=== idx: 56 ===
Gold supporting: these results suggest that uv treatment results in an induced repair of uv - damaged dna in the transcribed strand of an active gene in xp - c and normal cells through an enhancement of tcr or a mechanism which involves the tcr pathway.
LLM supporting:  in the context provided, the specific gene strand targeted by transcription - coupled repair ( tcr ) is not directly mentioned. however, based on the general principles of tcr, it is known that tcr primarily targets the non - template ( or leading ) dna strand. this is because tcr is initiated during the transcription process, where the rna polymerase temporarily pauses at the site of dna damage, allowing for the recruitment of repair factors to the leading strand. the leading strand is the one that is being synthesized and is therefore more susceptible to damage that can impede transcription. tcr is particularly efficient in repairing uv - induced dna lesions in active genes, as it is coupled to the transcription machinery, ensuring that the newly synthesized rna does not contain the same base misincorporation as the template. this mechanism is crucial for maintaining the integrity of the genetic information and preventing the transmission of errors in the transcribed mrnas.
